<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49402232' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://forum.level1techs.com/t/why-your-local-llm-feels-dumber-than-it-is/253917' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why your local LLM feels dumber than it is</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何本地大模型看起来比实际更笨</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49402232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 476</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49402232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 198</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post debunks the illusion that local LLMs are inherently smarter or more capable than they seem. It explains how constraints like small context windows, limited memory, and lack of tooling integration create a perception of incompetence, and offers practical fixes such as retrieval augmentation, better prompting, and hardware upgrades.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章揭穿本地大模型本质上并非更聪明的错觉，指出小上下文窗口、内存限制和缺乏工具集成等因素会让模型显得“笨”，并给出实际的改进办法，如检索增强、改进提示和硬件升级。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://level1techs.us-east-1.linodeobjects.com/original/4X/d/8/7/d87c01532439840126d90ea94abfdfcf6501f42f.png' data-full='https://level1techs.us-east-1.linodeobjects.com/original/4X/d/8/7/d87c01532439840126d90ea94abfdfcf6501f42f.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49400408' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://quantumi.sh/public/labs.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ElevenLabs, TwelveLabs, ThirteenLabs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>十一实验室、十二实验室、十三实验室</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49400408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 463</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49400408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 138</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise look at the trio of labs dubbed ElevenLabs, TwelveLabs, and ThirteenLabs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>简要聚焦名为 ElevenLabs、TwelveLabs 和 ThirteenLabs 的三家实验室。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49323381' data-tags='Business,Startups,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/08/16/stripe-will-reportedly-acquire-ai-gateway-startup-openrouter-for-7b/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stripe will reportedly acquire OpenRouter for $7B+</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Stripe 将以约 70 亿美元收购 OpenRouter</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49323381' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 463</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49323381' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 289</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stripe reportedly plans to acquire OpenRouter, an AI gateway startup, in a deal valued around $7B+. The move signals Stripe&#x27;s push into developer-facing AI tooling and model routing, potentially accelerating integration of AI into payments and commerce.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道 Stripe 将收购 AI 网关 OpenRouter，交易额约 70 亿美元。这笔交易凸显 Stripe 在面向开发者的 AI 工具与模型路由领域的布局，或将加速将 AI 融入支付与电商场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/04/Stripe-resized.webp?resize=1200,674' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/04/Stripe-resized.webp?resize=1200,674' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49354949' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cerebras.ai/cs4' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cerebras CS-4</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cerebras CS‑4</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49354949' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 459</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49354949' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 269</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Cerebras CS‑4 is the latest wafer‑scale AI accelerator offering massive on‑chip memory and compute for training very large models without heavy distributed overhead. Its architecture targets organizations building foundation models, promising throughput gains and simpler scaling compared with multi‑chip clusters, but at significant power and cost profiles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cerebras CS‑4 是新一代晶圓級 AI 加速器，提供巨量片上記憶體與運算能力，能在較少分散式開銷下訓練超大模型。對於構建基礎模型的機構來說，它在吞吐量與擴展性上有優勢，但也伴隨較高的功耗與成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/e4qjo92p/production/21fa43f6d541115e57887beb1155bddc7541ae71-2400x1260.jpg?w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;fit=max&amp;auto=format' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/e4qjo92p/production/21fa43f6d541115e57887beb1155bddc7541ae71-2400x1260.jpg?w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;fit=max&amp;auto=format' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49362887' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.casio.com/uk/watches/casio/product.F-B100W-1A/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Casio F-B100W-1A</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Casio 推出 F-B100W-1A 手表</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49362887' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 458</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49362887' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 381</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Casio unveils the F-B100W-1A, a continued entry in Casio’s line of reliable digital watches. The release illustrates how legacy hardware brands keep updating wearables in a competitive market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Casio 推出 F-B100W-1A 手表，延续其可靠数字表的系列。此举显示了传统硬件品牌在竞争激烈的可穿戴市场中持续迭代更新。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49351802' data-tags='Privacy,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hister.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hister – A private, full content search index that you control</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hister：一个私有、可控的全文检索索引</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49351802' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 454</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49351802' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hister provides a private, fully controllable content search index you can run yourself, letting you keep data in-house while still offering full-text search capabilities. It emphasizes data sovereignty and customizable indexing for private or enterprise use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hister 提供私有、可完全自控的全文检索索引，允许你在本地部署搜索能力而不将数据暴露给第三方。它强调数据主权与可定制的索引能力，适用于私有或企业场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hister.org/logo.png' data-full='https://hister.org/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49349984' data-tags='Design,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ikea.com/se/en/customer-service/knowledge/articles/6f564c4d-2ccc-46de-b643-545a3948dc79.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How does IKEA come up with names for its products?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>宜家如何為產品命名？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49349984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 454</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49349984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 323</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>IKEA uses a systematic naming convention—assigning Swedish words and categories to products based on type, taxonomy, and sometimes whimsical themes—rather than serial numbers. That approach simplifies cataloging across thousands of SKUs while creating memorable, brand‑specific names that double as identifiers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>宜家採用系統化的命名規則，依產品類型與分類以瑞典語單詞（有時帶有俏皮主題）來命名，而非純數字編碼。這種做法在數千個 SKU 中既便於分類管理，也能產生具品牌辨識度且易記的名稱。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.ikea.com/global/assets/logos/brand/ikea.svg' data-full='https://www.ikea.com/global/assets/logos/brand/ikea.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49363433' data-tags='Privacy,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/2013/11/civic-hygiene/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Civic Hygiene – avoid building technologies that could be used by a police state (2013)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>公民卫生：避免开发可能被警察国家利用的技术（2013）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49363433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 450</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49363433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 332</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2013 essay argues designers should avoid building tech that enables mass surveillance or state control. It offers a principled stance on responsible system design and privacy-preserving defaults.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇 2013 年的文章主张设计者应避免开发可能被大规模监控或国家控制用到的技术，强调负责任的系统设计和隐私保护的默认设置。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/wp-content/uploads/2013/03/trans-1792756_640.jpg' data-full='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/wp-content/uploads/2013/03/trans-1792756_640.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49348912' data-tags='Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/2026/08/and-then-the-men-with-guns-tell-you-to-do-it-anyway/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>And then the men with guns tell you to do it anyway</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>然後有拿槍的人告訴你還是照做</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49348912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 446</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49348912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 395</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Legal rights or principles can be rendered moot in practice when armed authorities compel a different action, underscoring the gap between theory and enforced reality. The sentence warns that coercive enforcement often determines outcomes regardless of legal correctness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>理論上的權利在面對持械執法時可能無法體現，強制力往往決定實際結果。這句話提醒人們：被執行的現實常常凌駕於法理之上。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/fire-alert.webp' data-full='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/fire-alert.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49344825' data-tags='Science,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://philo.gay/linecam/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using the railway network as a flatbed scanner</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把鐵路網當成平板掃描器使用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49344825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 442</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49344825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This project turns motion along railways into a giant line‑scanner: mounting a camera to capture sequential slices as trains move creates ultra‑wide, high‑resolution imagery of infrastructure. It&#x27;s an inventive hack that treats transportation motion as a sensing mechanism for mapping and visualization at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>該項目利用火車運動作為線掃描器：在列車上安裝攝影機，隨行進擷取序列切片，拼接出超寬高解析度的基礎設施影像。這種巧思將交通運動視為大尺度感測手段，用於地圖與視覺化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://philo.gay/linecam/blogpics_1x/cranes-edit.jpg' data-full='https://philo.gay/linecam/blogpics_1x/cranes-edit.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49368886' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/tech/steve-jobs-apple-next-cia-161b65f9?st=NWWds1&amp;reflink=desktopwebshare_permalink' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CIA funding helped keep NeXT afloat in the 80s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CIA资助在80年代帮助NeXT度过难关</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49368886' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 435</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49368886' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 257</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New reporting reveals the CIA provided funding that helped keep NeXT afloat in the 1980s, illustrating government involvement in early tech ventures. The piece sheds light on how defense-linked backing intersected with Silicon Valley and influenced Jobs’ trajectory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新报道披露，CIA 在1980年代提供资金，帮助 NeXT 存续。这揭示了政府资助在早期科技企业中的作用，以及国防利益如何影响硅谷格局与乔布斯的职业轨迹。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49319389' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=%22kidney+disappointment%22' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Research papers using &quot;kidney disappointment&quot; instead of &quot;kidney failure&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究论文把“kidney disappointment”代替“kidney failure”？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49319389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 434</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49319389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A linguistic note highlights papers using &#x27;kidney disappointment&#x27; instead of &#x27;kidney failure&#x27;, illustrating how terminology can soften or reframing research narratives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有论文把“kidney disappointment”用于替代“kidney failure”，以缓和叙事语气。该现象反映了语言选择如何影响患者感知与科学传播的方式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49405870' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nappertime.com/the-golden-rule-of-becoming-a-better-writer/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>To become a better writer, read as much as you can</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>多读书，写作更好</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49405870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 433</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49405870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 252</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advocates reading widely as the fastest path to improve writing, emphasizing deliberate reading, note-taking, and analyzing style. The piece offers practical steps to turn reading into better sentence craft and ideas.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张通过广泛阅读来提升写作能力，强调有目的的阅读、做笔记和分析风格的重要性，并给出将阅读转化为写作技巧的实用步骤。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nappertime.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/reading-books.png' data-full='https://nappertime.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/reading-books.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49361279' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.raphaelbauer.com:443/posts/postgresql-everything/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PostgreSQL for Everything</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PostgreSQL 万能化：成为全能数据平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49361279' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 433</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49361279' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 261</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An argument for treating PostgreSQL as a universal data platform, showcasing its capabilities beyond a traditional database—from JSON and GIS to advanced analytics. It surveys how PostgreSQL can be the backbone for many modern apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张将 PostgreSQL 当作通用数据平台使用，展示其超越传统数据库的能力，如 JSON、GIS 与高级分析等。探讨 PostgreSQL 如何成为许多现代应用的支柱。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.raphaelbauer.com/images/db-servers.jpg' data-full='https://www.raphaelbauer.com/images/db-servers.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://quantum5.ca/2026/08/16/fixing-bricked-amd-7040-series-framework-13-laptop-with-20-tools/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fixing a bricked Framework laptop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>修復磚化的 Framework 筆電</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49345220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 422</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49345220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 297</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hardware‑level walkthrough shows how to recover a bricked Framework 13 (AMD 7040 series) laptop using external programmers, test‑point access, and a detailed toolset. The guide highlights that modular, repairable designs still require advanced skills and hardware to fix firmware‑level failures, but recovery is possible with patience and the right equipment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>這篇硬體層級的教學示範如何用外接燒錄器、測試點與一套專門工具修復磚化的 Framework 13（AMD 7040）。它指出即便是可維修的模組化設計，遇到韌體級故障仍需要專業技能與儀器，但只要有耐心和合適工具是能夠恢復的。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wiz.io/blog/red-agent-snowflake-copilot-cicd-bug' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI-Generated GitHub Copilot “Autofix” Allowed Compromise of Snowflake&#x27;s Jira</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 生成的 Copilot 自动修复 使 Snowflake Jira 出现漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49331423' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 422</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49331423' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-generated Copilot autofix reportedly enabled a security compromise in Snowflake’s Jira instance, illustrating how automation can introduce new attack paths in CI/CD and issue tracking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称 AI 生成的 Copilot 自动修复导致 Snowflake 的 Jira 出现安全漏洞，揭示自动化在持续集成/持续交付和问题追踪中可能带来的新攻击路径。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rust-glancer.github.io/blog/hello-world/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rust Glancer: Rust LSP using 100x less RAM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust Glancer：Rust LSP 将 RAM 使用量降低百倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49393052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 412</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49393052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rust Glancer introduces a Rust language server that uses dramatically less RAM, enabling smoother IDE experiences for large codebases. The project demonstrates how rethinking data structures and indexing can shrink memory footprints without sacrificing responsiveness. This could unlock Rust tooling on limited hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rust Glancer 提供一个内存使用量极低的 Rust 语言服务器，使大型代码库在集成开发环境中更流畅。该项目通过重新设计数据结构和索引降低了内存占用，同时保持响应性，有望在资源受限的硬件上提升 Rust 的开发体验。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c1l1r1zne1ro' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Children&#x27;s stunted lungs show recovery in ultra low emission zone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>儿童肺部发育在超低排放区显示恢复</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49355105' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 401</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49355105' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 388</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study finds children&#x27;s lung development shows recovery after exposure reduction in ultra-low emission zones, underscoring public-health benefits of environmental policy. The results support urban planning strategies that limit pollution exposure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现，在超低排放区减少暴露后，儿童肺部发育显现恢复，凸显环境政策对公共健康的益处。研究结果支持以降低污染暴露为目标的城市规划策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/4dc6/live/93fc0f30-9ad4-11f1-aed2-8d6da8d75094.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/4dc6/live/93fc0f30-9ad4-11f1-aed2-8d6da8d75094.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sockpuppet.org/blog/2026/08/20/stop-making-tuis/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stop Making TUIs</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49384210' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 393</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49384210' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 512</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take arguing against building text-based UIs in favor of more modern interfaces. It weighs the UX trade-offs of TUIs and suggests leaning toward graphical or web-enabled designs to improve accessibility and productivity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对是否继续开发文本用户界面提出质疑，强调采用更现代的界面以提升可用性与生产力。评估了 TUIs 的用户体验权衡，建议倾向于图形化或网页化的设计方案。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.fairphone.com/nl/stories/the-fairphone-gen-6-is-all-about-giving-you-more' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fairphone is now officially available in the United States</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fairphone 正式在美國上市</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49344811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 391</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49344811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 195</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fairphone&#x27;s Gen 6 is now officially sold in the U.S., giving American consumers access to a modular, repairable smartphone designed for longer lifecycles and improved supply‑chain ethics. Availability signals growing market appetite for sustainable hardware, though distribution and after‑sales ecosystems will determine mainstream traction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fairphone 第六代已在美國正式販售，讓美國消費者能買到以模組化與可維修性為核心、注重供應鏈倫理與延長使用壽命的手機。此舉顯示市場對永續硬體的需求上升，但能否普及還取決於銷售與售後服務網絡的完整度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.fairphone.com/web/content/fairphone.story/192/thumbnail/FP6+Launch-Blog-Header.jpg' data-full='https://www.fairphone.com/web/content/fairphone.story/192/thumbnail/FP6+Launch-Blog-Header.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49355142' data-tags='Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bfi.uchicago.edu/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/BFI_WP_2026-108-1.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sticky wage norms and the real wage cost of unexpected inflation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>僵化工資規範與意外通膨的實際工資成本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49355142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 385</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49355142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 272</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper models how sticky wage norms affect employers&#x27; real labor costs when inflation surprises occur, finding that wage rigidities can shift much of the burden away from firms and onto workers via eroded real wages. Quantifying that transfer helps explain labor‑market frictions and informs policy on inflation stabilization and wage indexation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>該研究建模分析在意外通貨膨脹下僵化工資規範如何影響雇主的實際勞動成本，發現工資黏性會透過實質薪資下降將負擔大量轉嫁給勞工。對這種轉移進行量化有助理解勞動市場摩擦，並為通膨穩定與工資指數化政策提供依據。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49353221' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/DenisSergeevitch/desktop-fly' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A 3D fruit fly on macOS desktop powered by the real FlyWire connectome</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以真實 FlyWire 連接體為動力的 macOS 桌面 3D 果蠅</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49353221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 374</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49353221' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 184</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This macOS widget renders a 3D fruit‑fly on the desktop whose neuronal activity is driven by the FlyWire connectome data, turning real connectomic wiring into live visual behavior. It&#x27;s a playful but impressive demo of making scientific connectome datasets interactive and tangible on a personal computer.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>這個 macOS 小工具在桌面上呈現一隻 3D 果蠅，其神經活動由 FlyWire 連接體資料驅動，將真實的連接體網絡轉化為即時可視的行為。這是把科學連接體資料互動化並帶到個人電腦上的趣味而令人印象深刻的示範。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/16f506e97fb29db92b0a5585c8c75e00a70ec6c2cd32a605a55d5f0469349ba3/DenisSergeevitch/desktop-fly' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/16f506e97fb29db92b0a5585c8c75e00a70ec6c2cd32a605a55d5f0469349ba3/DenisSergeevitch/desktop-fly' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49378768' data-tags='AI,Programming,Show HN'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.danielvaughn.dev/posts/huzzah/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Huzzah – a novel approach to coding with AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Huzzah——一种以 AI 编码的新方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49378768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 372</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49378768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 207</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Huzzah presents a new approach to coding with AI, aiming to streamline developer flow and reduce cognitive load through integrated AI assistance. It showcases how tooling can shift from traditional autocompletion to creative AI-assisted programming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Huzzah 提供了一种以 AI 辅助编码的新方法，力图通过集成的 AI 助手简化开发流程、减轻认知负担。它体现了工具链如何从简单的自动完成转向更具创造性的 AI 辅助编程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.danielvaughn.dev/opengraph-image.jpg' data-full='https://www.danielvaughn.dev/opengraph-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49357530' data-tags='AI,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/graphic-detail/2026/08/18/does-ai-stop-children-from-learning' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI boosted homework scores, then exam scores dropped: study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 提升作业分数后考试分数下降：一项研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49357530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 370</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49357530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 371</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study finds AI-assisted homework boosts grades, but exam scores later drop, suggesting that AI helps with surface tasks while undermining deeper understanding. The result raises questions about how to design assessments and keep learning progress intact in AI-enabled classrooms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现 AI 辅助的作业分数上升，但随后的考试成绩下降，提示 AI 可能提升“表面”任务的表现而影响深层理解。该现象引发对 AI 时代课程评估设计的反思，如何在不损害学习深度的前提下使用 AI。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A historical look at Japan&#x27;s ambitious OS project and US government intervention, revealing how geopolitics shapes tech ambitions. The episode underscores the fragility of national projects when global power dynamics come into play.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾日本雄心勃勃的全球化操作系统计划及美国政府介入，揭示地缘政治如何影响技术野心。此事凸显在全球权力格局作用下，国家级技术项目的脆弱性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://risky.biz/risky-bulletin-slovakia-finds-russian-backdoor-in-traffic-speed-cameras/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Slovakia finds Russian backdoor in traffic speed cameras</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>斯洛伐克在交通测速摄像头中发现俄方后门</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49409200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 363</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49409200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Russian backdoor was found in Slovakia&#x27;s traffic speed cameras, highlighting cybersecurity risks in critical infrastructure and the need for secure supply chains and ongoing monitoring. The incident underscores why hardware and firmware provenance matter for national resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>斯洛伐克的交通测速摄像头中发现了俄方后门，凸显关键基础设施的网络安全风险以及对安全供应链和持续监控的需求。这一事件强调了硬件与固件来源对国家韧性的意义。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A take that GPT-5.6 Sol represents OpenAI’s best-performing vision-capable model to date, highlighting progress in multimodal AI and potential use cases in image understanding, captioning, and more.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/adnanakil/nobuzz/blob/main/README.md' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claudette: Make Claude stop talking like a BuzzFeed article</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49388752' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 352</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49388752' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 235</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claudette proposes filters to reduce Claude&#x27;s BuzzFeed-like tone, advocating for more neutral and concise AI responses. The post hints at tooling to shape AI persona for clearer, more utilitarian outputs. It reflects ongoing efforts to tame AI ergonomics for practical use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claudette 提议通过过滤器调整 Claude 的语气，减少像 BuzzFeed 那样的风格，追求更中性和简洁的回答。文中暗示了通过工具塑造 AI 人设以获得更实用的输出，体现了让 AI 更易用的持续努力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rathbiotaclan.com/tiktok-videos-deactivate-key-cognitive-brain-regions/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Watching TikTok and Instagram deactivates the cognitive control network: Study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究称观看 TikTok 与 Instagram 会抑制认知控制网络</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49378630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 352</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49378630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 120</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study links frequent short-video consumption to reduced activity in brain regions involved in cognitive control and self-regulation. The findings raise questions about platform design and long-term attention, with potential implications for education and mental health.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究将频繁观看短视频与大脑在认知控制和自控相关的区域活性下降联系起来。研究结果引发对平台设计、长期注意力和教育/心理健康影响的讨论，提示需要更谨慎的设计与使用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://annas-archive.pk/blog/physical-destruction.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI companies destroy physical books – let&#x27;s scan rare books before it&#x27;s too late</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Same as earlier call to digitize rare books before AI-driven data usage erases them; emphasizes balancing copyright, access, and preservation as technology advances.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>同上，呼吁在 AI 训练时代到来前对稀有书籍进行数字化保存，平衡数字化与版权等问题，避免珍贵藏品随时间流失。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.modular.com/blog/mojo-open-source' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mojo is now open source</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mojo 现已开源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49348079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 350</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49348079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mojo, a high-performance language blending Python-like syntax with low-level capability, has been open-sourced. The release expands options for building AI workloads that demand both developer ergonomics and speed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mojo 这门结合了 Python 风格语法和低级能力的高性能语言已开源。此举为需要在易用性与性能之间取得平衡的 AI 工作负载提供了新的选择。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.computerenhance.com/p/turns-are-better-than-radians' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Turns are Better than Radians (2022)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Turns 比弧度更好（2022）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49369408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 344</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49369408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 208</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An argument that using turns (full rotations) instead of radians can reduce arithmetic errors and improve readability in certain algorithms. The piece encourages considering alternative angle representations in math-heavy code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出在某些算法中使用“圈数”而非弧度可以降低运算错误并提升可读性。文章鼓励在数学密集的代码中考虑替代的角度表示法。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide to reducing exposure to intrusive AI features, with privacy-first strategies and tooling to opt out of tracking and data collection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>实用指南，介绍如何减少暴露于侵入式 AI 的影响，提供隐私优先的策略和避免追踪的数据工具。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49322695' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://w4g1.dev/blog/models-are-getting-dumber-on-purpose' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Models Are Getting Dumber on Purpose</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>模型在有意降级以提升安全性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49322695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 333</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49322695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that AI models are getting intentionally dumber to improve safety, control, or cost, rather than simply getting smarter from more data. It discusses the tension between capability and governance, and what that means for developers relying on ever more powerful systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为，AI 模型正被有意降级，以提高安全性、可控性以及成本效益，而不仅仅是靠更多数据来提升能力。讨论了能力与治理之间的张力，以及开发者在日益强大的系统面前所面临的取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://w4g1.dev/avatar.webp' data-full='https://w4g1.dev/avatar.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jsomers.net/i-should-have-loved-biology/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I should have loved biology (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2020 年我本该爱上生物学</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49377853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 332</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49377853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflective piece about why biology deserves more attention in tech culture and how cross-disciplinary curiosity can enrich problem solving. It argues that biology offers powerful metaphors and real-world lessons for software, AI, and systems thinking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇反思文章讨论为何生物学在科技文化中应获得更多关注，以及跨学科求知如何丰富问题解决能力。作者认为生物学能为软件、AI 与系统思维提供有力的类比与现实启示。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/kuberwastaken/status/2089377982536388964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude writing a macOS driver for my obscure HP printer built only for Windows</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 為我僅供 Windows 的罕見 HP 印表機撰寫 macOS 驅動</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49344643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 327</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49344643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 222</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI (Claude) generated a macOS driver for an obscure HP printer that originally only supported Windows, demonstrating how large models can assist in reverse‑engineering and driver creation. The example showcases practical AI‑assisted engineering for niche hardware, but also raises questions about correctness, testing, and maintainability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一則示例顯示 AI（Claude）為一款原本只支援 Windows 的罕見 HP 印表機生成了 macOS 驅動，證明大型模型能協助逆向工程與驅動開發。這凸顯了 AI 在利基硬體上提供實務協助的潛力，但也帶出正確性、測試與可維護性的疑慮。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HP72MdaaEAAAX7z.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HP72MdaaEAAAX7z.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49320611' data-tags='Business,Startups,AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vectoral.com/blog/who-are-the-token-brokers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The AI Credit Resale Economy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 计算信用再销售经济</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49320611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 327</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49320611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An emerging market is forming around trading AI compute credits and usage rights, with token brokers providing liquidity and signaling prices. The system could lower the barrier to AI access for startups and individuals, but it also invites resale scams and regulatory scrutiny as standards and protections emerge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个围绕AI计算信用和使用权买卖的新兴市场正在形成，经纪人聚合流动性并传递价格信号。该模式可能降低创业公司和个人获得AI能力的门槛，但随着标准化和保护机制尚未完善，也引发转卖欺诈与监管关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.ploy.ai/2eb2235a-6b78-4d32-9713-f051311d8809/user/c6ffdb67-screenshot-2026-08-10-at-3.18.58-pm.webp' data-full='https://cdn.ploy.ai/2eb2235a-6b78-4d32-9713-f051311d8809/user/c6ffdb67-screenshot-2026-08-10-at-3.18.58-pm.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.xda-developers.com/qwen-3-8-27b-reverse-engineering-job-frontier-model/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I gave Qwen 3.8 27B a reverse-engineering job and it finished in 30 minutes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我给 Qwen 3.8 27B 指派做反向工程任务，30 分钟就完成</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49407507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 318</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49407507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hands-on test shows a 27B LLM can perform a reverse-engineering task quickly, highlighting AI assistance for code analysis and problem solving. It demonstrates potential speedups in tasks like malware analysis or binary comprehension, while reminding that verification and safety checks remain essential.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>亲身测试显示 Qwen 3.8 27B 能在短时间内完成反向工程任务，展现了 AI 对代码分析与问题求解的辅助潜力。虽然可能带来分析效率的提升，但仍需谨慎验证并注意安全与准确性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/business/2026/08/18/metas-blockbuster-trial-draws-parallels-to-big-tobacco' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta&#x27;s blockbuster trial draws parallels to big tobacco</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 的重磅審判被比擬為大型煙草公司案件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49355825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 313</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49355825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 296</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The trial frames Meta&#x27;s conduct in a manner reminiscent of big‑tobacco litigation—alleging the company knew about harms from its products and prioritized growth over safety. The comparison raises stakes about internal research, regulatory scrutiny, and potential remedies if the court finds corporate culpability similar to past public‑health cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>審判將 Meta 的行為比作大型煙草案件，指控公司可能知悉其產品造成的傷害卻優先追求成長。這種比擬提高了內部研究揭露、監管審查與若法院認定公司有責任時可能採取補救措施的嚴重性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49349147' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://asmedigitalcollection.asme.org/sustainablebuildings/article/7/2/024501/1233035/Data-Center-Waste-Heat-as-an-Emerging-Urban' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Field measurements of neighborhood-scale air temperature impacts of data centers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>資料中心對鄰里尺度空氣溫度影響的實地測量</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49349147' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 311</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49349147' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 497</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Field measurements show that data centers&#x27; waste heat can raise neighborhood‑scale air temperatures, contributing to local urban warming. The study quantifies the magnitude and spatial footprint of that effect, informing siting decisions and the need to consider waste‑heat management in urban planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>實地觀測顯示資料中心排放的廢熱會使周邊社區的空氣溫度上升，加劇局部的城市熱化。該研究量化了影響的強度與空間範圍，為選址決策與城市規劃中廢熱管理提供依據。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.andros.dev/blog/54572bc7/finger-the-1971-social-network-that-never-died/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Finger: the 1971 social network that never died</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49342472' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 311</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49342472' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 110</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Finger, an early online social networking protocol from 1971, persisted in niche communities and influenced later identity services. The piece traces its lineage from ARPANET roots to modern social computing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾性文章讲述 Finger——1971 年的早期在线社交网络，尽管时间久远却在部分社区依然存在并影响了后来的身份服务。文章梳理了它从 ARPANET 起源到现代社交计算的演变脉络。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/zachahn/vomit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vomit: Clean up Claude 5&#x27;s token output with a separate LLM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vomit：用单独的 LLM 清理 Claude 5 的输出</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49375996' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 299</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49375996' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 291</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vomit proposes using a separate LLM to post-process Claude 5&#x27;s token output, aiming to reduce hallucinations and improve reliability. The approach illustrates how modular safety pipelines can complement the base model.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vomit 提出用独立的语言模型对 Claude 5 的输出进行后处理，以降低幻觉率并提高可靠性。这一做法展示了模块化安全管线如何与基础模型协同提升安全性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49349898' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/RyanCodrai/turbovec' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Turbovec – Google&#x27;s TurboQuant for vector search in Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Turbovec：用 Rust 實作的 Google TurboQuant 向量搜尋</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49349898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 296</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49349898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Turbovec is a Rust implementation of TurboQuant for compressed vector search, aiming to speed up nearest‑neighbor queries with lower memory footprints. It&#x27;s useful for embedding search workflows that need efficient on‑disk or in‑memory storage with fast distance computations in production systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Turbovec 是用 Rust 實作的 TurboQuant 向量搜尋庫，致力於在降低記憶體使用的同時加速最近鄰查詢。對需在生產環境中以高效方式儲存嵌入並快速計算距離的應用來說十分實用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1192555574/000b661c-3519-42a5-9609-d5051c50c2f0' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1192555574/000b661c-3519-42a5-9609-d5051c50c2f0' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49345843' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theconversation.com/babies-born-under-sugar-rationing-grew-into-adults-with-lower-cancer-risk-289873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Babies born under sugar rationing grew into adults with lower cancer risk</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在糖類配給期間出生的嬰兒長大後罹癌率較低</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49345843' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 295</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49345843' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>People born during historical sugar rationing periods showed lower adult cancer rates, suggesting early‑life nutrition affects long‑term cancer risk. The finding adds to evidence that prenatal and childhood dietary environments can have lasting epidemiological consequences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在糖類配給期間出生的人長大後罹癌率較低，顯示早年營養環境會影響長期癌症風險。這一結論補強了產前與兒童期飲食會對人口健康產生持久影響的證據。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.theconversation.com/files/754389/original/file-20260817-50-s3fxyn.jpg?ixlib=rb-4.1.1&amp;rect=0%2C389%2C5086%2C2543&amp;q=45&amp;auto=format&amp;w=1356&amp;h=668&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://images.theconversation.com/files/754389/original/file-20260817-50-s3fxyn.jpg?ixlib=rb-4.1.1&amp;rect=0%2C389%2C5086%2C2543&amp;q=45&amp;auto=format&amp;w=1356&amp;h=668&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.xda-developers.com/25-year-old-brazilian-video-patent-expired-legal-headache-linux/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A 25-year-old video patent just expired, ending a legal headache for Linux</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>25 岁的视频专利到期，结束 Linux 的法律麻烦</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49311814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 295</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49311814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A long-standing Brazilian patent related to video technology has expired, removing a legal hurdle for Linux-based video playback and distribution. The expiry could simplify patent risk calculations for distributions and boost compatibility for multimedia stacks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项与视频技术相关的巴西专利已于今日到期，消除了 Linux 相关的法律障碍。到期可能简化发行版在多媒体编解码方面的专利风险评估，并提升兼容性与部署便利性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static0.xdaimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/wm/2026/06/img_4724.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://static0.xdaimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/wm/2026/06/img_4724.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A community-edited wiki covering consumer rights, digital privacy, and data governance topics relevant to tech users and developers. It serves as a practical, crowd-sourced resource for understanding your rights online.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个由社区维护的百科，聚焦消费者权利、数字隐私和数据治理等与科技用户密切相关的议题，帮助公众了解网络权利与保护措施。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49348751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 292</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49348751' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 261</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>There&#x27;s a limited‑time promotion increasing weekly usage limits for Claude Code users from May through August 2026, temporarily raising the available compute or token caps. Users should check the exact week‑by‑week limits and expiration to take advantage during the promotional window.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 在 2026 年 5 到 8 月提供限時週用量上限提升的促銷，暫時增加可用的運算或 token 限額。使用者需留意各週的具體上限與期限，以便在促銷期內充分利用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lalitm.com/post/find-problems-staff-engineer/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How I find problems to solve as a staff engineer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我在担任资深工程师时如何发现待解决的问题</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Learn a practical framework for identifying high-leverage problems as a staff engineer by combining system signals, user pain points, and business impact. The piece emphasizes partnering with product and ops, triaging by impact and effort, and turning findings into scoped initiatives with measurable outcomes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文介绍在担任资深工程师时如何通过系统信号、用户痛点和业务影响来发现高杠杆的问题，提供实用框架。强调与产品和运维协作、按影响与投入进行优先级排序，并将发现转化为有明确可衡量目标的计划。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/08/apple-announces-changes-for-apps-in-the-european-union/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple announces changes for apps in the European Union</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蘋果宣布針對歐盟應用程式的變更</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49348055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 283</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49348055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 432</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple announced App Store changes for the EU to comply with regional regulations, loosening some platform restrictions like alternative app distribution and payment options. The updates aim to balance regulatory compliance with platform security and business models, but details of implementation and developer costs will determine their real impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>蘋果針對歐盟發布了 App Store 的變更，以符合當地法規，放寬了部分限制，例如允許替代應用分發與支付選項。這些更新試圖在遵法、平台安全與商業模式間取得平衡，但實際影響取決於落地細節與對開發者的成本影響。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/08/apple-announces-changes-for-apps-in-the-european-union/tile/Apple-App-Store-compliance-hero-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202608181559' data-full='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/08/apple-announces-changes-for-apps-in-the-european-union/tile/Apple-App-Store-compliance-hero-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202608181559' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新世界：我们正生活在 J. G. Ballard 或 William Gibson 的未来之中</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49387525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 277</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49387525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 180</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece argues that our daily reality already echoes the dystopian futures imagined by J. G. Ballard and William Gibson, blending surveillance, technology, and urban decay. It invites readers to rethink whether the present is a prelude to science-fiction becoming ordinary.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章认为，我们的日常现实已在某种程度上呼应 J. G. Ballard 与威廉·吉布森笔下的反乌托邦未来，技术、监控与城市衰败交织。让读者重新思考如今是否正成为科幻走向现实的序幕。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://precastreinforced.co.uk/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/delivery_robot.jpg' data-full='https://precastreinforced.co.uk/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/delivery_robot.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49410362' data-tags='Open Source,Web,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://debloat.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A website for debloated open source alternatives</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>去臃肿的开源替代品网站</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49410362' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 275</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49410362' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Debloat.dev curates lighter, less feature-heavy open-source replacements for popular apps, aiming to reduce resource usage without sacrificing utility. The site helps users bypass bloatware and regain control over performance, privacy, and autonomy. For power users and older devices, it can be a practical shortcut to leaner software choices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Debloat.dev 汇集了主流应用的更轻量、功能负担较低的开源替代品，目标是减少资源占用同时保留实用性。该站点帮助用户规避臃肿软件，提升性能、隐私与自主性。对于追求高效的高级用户和老旧设备，这是一条实用的替代路径。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bruce Eckel 的 Thinking in Python 仍是 Python 学习者的权威资源，深入探讨语言特性、设计模式与惯用法。该资料为初学者与有经验的开发者提供实用视角，帮助用更地道的方式编写 Python。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://linear.axler.net/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linear algebra done right</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>线性代数的正确做法——Axler 著</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49326816' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 263</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49326816' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 107</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Axler argues for a streamlined, proof-oriented treatment of linear algebra that starts with vector spaces and linear maps, not determinants. The book emphasizes intuition and structure over computational recipes, helping readers grasp spectral theory earlier and more cleanly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Axler 主张以向量空间与线性变换为出发点的线性代数教学，摒弃以行列式为中心的传统做法，强调直觉与结构，较早引入特征值与谱理论，帮助读者建立清晰的理论框架。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://linear.axler.net/coverLADR4e.png' data-full='https://linear.axler.net/coverLADR4e.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.privacyguides.org/news/2026/08/17/meta-files-patent-for-facial-recognition-automatic-recording-of-people/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta Files Patent for Facial Recognition, Automatic Recording of People</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 申請臉部辨識與自動錄影專利</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49344654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 262</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49344654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 181</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A newly filed patent describes systems for facial recognition combined with automatic recording of people, which could automate capture and tagging in physical spaces. Such technology raises privacy and surveillance concerns, especially around consent, data retention, and potential misuse in public or private settings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一項新申請的專利描述了結合臉部辨識與自動錄影的系統，可在實體空間中自動化捕捉與標記人物。這類技術在同意、資料保存和濫用風險方面引發隱私與監控的疑慮，無論在公共或私人場域皆需慎重考量。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.experimental-history.com/p/why-arent-smart-people-happier' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why aren&#x27;t smart people happier? (2022)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么聪明人不更快乐？（2022）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49378446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 260</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49378446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 407</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look at research suggesting that high intelligence doesn&#x27;t guarantee happiness, exploring factors like burnout, social isolation, and expectations. For tech readers, it underscores mental health considerations in high-performance fields.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示高智商未必带来更高幸福感，探讨烧脑工作、社交隔离和过高期望等因素。对科技从业者而言，这提醒关注高强度工作环境中的心理健康和生活平衡。</p>
