<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://newscenter.lbl.gov/2026/08/10/scientists-release-biggest-2d-map-of-the-universe/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scientists release biggest 2D map of the universe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科学家发布最大的二维宇宙地图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49392200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 258</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49392200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astronomers unveiled the largest 2D map of the universe, enabling new insights into cosmic structure, dark matter distribution, and galaxy evolution. The map synthesizes data across surveys to provide a richer baseline for cosmology. This milestone could accelerate research and inspire new instrumentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>天体物理学家公布了迄今最大的二维宇宙地图，为理解宇宙结构、暗物质分布和星系演化提供新视角。该地图整合了多项观测数据，建立更丰富的宇宙学基线。这一里程碑或促进相关研究的加速发展并激发新一轮仪器建设。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://newscenter.lbl.gov/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/Newscenter_Feature_1400px-DR11-M96_DECam_TAR-CC9.jpg' data-full='https://newscenter.lbl.gov/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/Newscenter_Feature_1400px-DR11-M96_DECam_TAR-CC9.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49351330' data-tags='Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.onethousandmeans.com/p/norway-should-buy-openai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Norway should buy OpenAI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>挪威應該買下 OpenAI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49351330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 257</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49351330' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 269</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The proposal argues that Norway—or another public actor—should acquire OpenAI to steer advanced AI toward public interest goals rather than purely commercial incentives. Advocates claim national ownership could align priorities on safety, transparency, and equitable benefit distribution, though the logistics and political feasibility remain major hurdles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>該主張認為挪威或其他公共實體應收購 OpenAI，以將先進 AI 的發展導向公共利益，而非僅受商業驅動。支持者認為國有化能調整安全性、透明度與利益分配的優先順序，但在執行面與政治可行性上仍有諸多障礙。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!kfb6!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F10158e80-df81-412d-ba5e-acefd5e353ad_2560x1920.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!kfb6!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F10158e80-df81-412d-ba5e-acefd5e353ad_2560x1920.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(137) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tau.dev/2026/08/07/canon' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Teaching my kid to code with a modern MUD</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用现代 MUD 教孩子编程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49272631' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 256</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49272631' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A parent&#x27;s approach to teaching coding through a modern MUD, blending storytelling with hands-on programming tasks. It demonstrates how interactive game worlds can make programming concepts tangible for kids.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以现代 MUD 为载体教学编程的家长实践，结合叙事与动手任务，展示互动游戏世界如何让孩子更好地理解编程与系统思维。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(138) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://geometridae.bearblog.dev/a-friendly-introduction-to-racket/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Friendly Introduction to Racket</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Racket友好入门</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49399898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 255</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49399898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Racket is a modern Lisp-derived language oriented toward education and scripting. This friendly introduction covers core syntax, REPL workflows, and practical tips to get started. It helps readers see where Racket shines compared with other languages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Racket 是一种源自 Lisp 的现代语言，面向教育和脚本编写。本文以友好的方式覆盖核心语法、REPL 工作流和入门要点，帮助读者了解 Racket 相较其他语言的优势。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bun 1.4 brings performance tweaks, boosted tooling, and compatibility improvements to the JavaScript runtime. The release enhances startup times, improves the bundler, and strengthens Node-style workflows for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bun 1.4 在 JavaScript 运行时方面带来启动更快、打包工具改进和兼容性增强等更新。该版本提升了开发体验，提供更高效的工作流，适合寻求更轻量工具链的开发者。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>解锁被锁定/停用的 Cricut Maker</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49365841' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 254</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49365841' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes approaches to unlock or reactivate a Cricut Maker that was locked or deactivated, highlighting DRM and device-management challenges. It discusses the tension between ownership, vendor controls, and legitimate user needs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍解锁或重新激活被锁定/停用的 Cricut Maker 的方法，突出数字版权管理与设备管理方面的挑战。讨论所有权、厂商控制与用户需求之间的张力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.modelcontextprotocol.io/posts/mcp-roadmap/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New MCP Roadmap</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MCP 路线图新展望</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49399591' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 252</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49399591' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 151</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The MCP roadmap details upcoming milestones for standardizing the Model Context Protocol, outlining phased releases, interoperability goals, and design decisions to improve cross-app context sharing. It highlights how MCP could enable more composable AI systems and privacy-aware data handling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MCP 路线图揭示标准化模型上下文协议的未来里程碑，包含分阶段发布、互操作性目标及关键设计选择，旨在提升跨应用的上下文共享。此处也强调该规范有望让AI系统更加可组合，并加强数据隐私与安全性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.benjoffe.com/fast-day-of-week' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A faster way to calculate the day of the week</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>更快的日期星期计算方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49323795' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 251</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49323795' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A faster method to compute the day of the week is presented, reducing branches and using simple arithmetic. The optimization benefits date-heavy workloads by lowering CPU cycles and latency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出一种更快的日期星期计算方法，减少分支、使用简单运算。对于大量日期处理场景，可以降低 CPU 周期和延迟。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.benjoffe.com/manual-assets/fast-day-of-week-socialthumb.png' data-full='https://www.benjoffe.com/manual-assets/fast-day-of-week-socialthumb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49409473' data-tags='Science,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://how.complexsystems.fail/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Complex Systems Fail (1998)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>复杂系统如何失效（1998）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49409473' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 250</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49409473' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper exposes how complex socio-technical systems fail through hidden interactions and cascading errors, not just individual faults. It argues for redesigns that emphasize safety culture, simple explanations for failures, and robust risk management—lessons that still apply to modern software and infrastructure. Understanding these dynamics helps teams anticipate cascades and improve resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该论文揭示了复杂社会技术系统如何通过隐藏的相互作用和级联错误而失败，而非仅仅是个别故障。它主张在设计上强调安全文化、对故障的简单解释以及强健的风险管理——这些教训至今仍适用于现代软件和基础设施。理解这些动力学有助于团队预测级联效应并提升韧性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49342530' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://acadia.engineering/blog/rethinking-database-programming' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rethinking Database Programming</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>重新思考資料庫程式設計</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49342530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 248</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49342530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues for shifting database programming toward higher‑level, developer-friendly abstractions that reduce impedance between application logic and data operations. Emphasizing composability, better type safety, and clearer semantics can help developers write safer, more maintainable data‑intensive code without wrestling raw SQL or brittle ORMs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主張將資料庫程式設計提升到更高階、對開發者友善的抽象層，以減少應用邏輯與資料操作之間的不協調。強調可組合性、更好的型別安全與清晰語意，有助於開發者在不必苦鬥原始 SQL 或脆弱 ORM 的情況下撰寫更可靠且易維護的資料密集型程式碼。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49323686' data-tags='Business,AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/nvidia-scales-back-250-billion-openai-data-center-guarantee-wsj-reports-2026-08-14/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia dramatically reduces amount of OpenAI infra financing it may guarantee</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英伟达大幅削减对 OpenAI 基础设施融资担保金额</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49323686' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 245</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49323686' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 151</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA is scaling back the amount of OpenAI infrastructure financing it may guarantee, trimming the potential-backed spend on data-center deployments. The move reflects risk management as AI workloads scale and could influence OpenAI’s ability to rapidly scale its compute needs. It signals a shift in how AI ecosystem players back AI workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英伟达大幅削减对 OpenAI 基础设施融资担保的金额，减少对数据中心部署的潜在背书支出。这一举措反映了在 AI 工作负载规模化过程中的风险管理，也可能影响 OpenAI 快速扩展算力的能力，显示出 AI 生态参与者背书 AI 工作负载的策略正在发生变化。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49325789' data-tags='AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reticulum is a decentralized mesh network project aiming to provide resilient, censorship-resistant communications without relying on centralized infrastructure. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Reticulum 是一个去中心化的网状网络项目，致力于在不依赖集中基础设施的情况下提供稳健、抗审查的通信。它强调隐私友好的路由和离线优先能力，使设备能够在临时网络中直接互联。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出把中间令牌当作推理痕迹会误导，讨论如何正确评估推理过程并避免过度解读。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49399524' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://decodingvibes.com/blog/a-kantian-critique-of-sorry-by-justin-bieber/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Kantian Critique of &quot;Sorry&quot; by Justin Bieber</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对贾斯汀·比伯《Sorry》的康德式批判</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49399524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 221</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49399524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 95</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An essay applies Kantian ethics to Justin Bieber&#x27;s apology in Sorry, arguing that a genuine apology must align with moral law and express true contrition rather than performative sentiment. The piece uses a pop-song moment to reflect on how sincerity, autonomy, and duty shape modern apologies in a tech-driven, social-media era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文将康德伦理学应用到贾斯汀·比伯的《Sorry》道歉之中，主张真正的道歉应符合道德法则并表达真实悔意，而非仅仅是表面的情感表现。它借助这段流行文化时刻，探讨在科技驱动、社交媒体盛行的时代，真诚、自治与义务如何塑造现代道歉。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49320984' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/money/2026/aug/16/the-weekend-is-100-years-old-skiveday-fridays-and-hybrid-working-ruined-it' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The weekend is 100 years old</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>周末已满100岁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49320984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 219</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49320984' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The weekend&#x27;s century-long history shows how factory schedules, rest days, and social norms shaped modern time off. Today, digital work, &#x27;skiveday&#x27; culture, and hybrid schedules are blurring or eroding traditional weekends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>周末的百年历史揭示了工作与休息的社会规则如何形成。如今数字化工作、灵活排班和混合办公正在改变或模糊传统周末的边界。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/46f90b7d7ffb12ee9deb1ede591298c3fa6893e8/0_1121_4847_3877/master/4847.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=c84ae6108d8b897afdc4c2a8bc503568' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/46f90b7d7ffb12ee9deb1ede591298c3fa6893e8/0_1121_4847_3877/master/4847.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=c84ae6108d8b897afdc4c2a8bc503568' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://support.google.com/photos/thread/256212140/how-do-i-permanently-disable-google-photos-pop-up-prompt-to-backup-my-photos-i?hl=en' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How do I permanently disable random Google Photos popup to backup photos? (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何永久关闭 Google 照片随机弹出以备份照片？（2024）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49290545' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 218</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49290545' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 166</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The thread discusses how to disable the backup prompt in Google Photos, outlining steps such as muting prompts, turning off backup &amp; sync, or adjusting notification settings. It clarifies trade-offs between convenience and privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>帖子给出关闭 Google 照片备份弹窗的做法，包含静音、关闭备份与同步、调整通知等步骤，并点出便利性与隐私之间的权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49408550' data-tags='Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://securelist.com/android-head-unit-malware/121106/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Malware infects Android-based automotive head unit firmware</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Android 汽车中控固件被木马感染</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49408550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 217</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49408550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 107</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Malware has compromised an Android-based automotive head unit, showing that infotainment firmware can be a doorway into a car&#x27;s ecosystem. The attack likely leverages insecure update pathways or compromised apps, enabling persistence and potential control of connected features. This case underscores the need for stronger supply-chain security, code signing, and robust OTA verification in modern vehicles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现基于 Android 的汽车中控固件被木马入侵，显现信息娱乐系统也可能成为进入车载生态的入口。攻击链可能利用不安全的更新机制或被篡改的应用，从而实现持久化和对连接功能的潜在控制。这凸显了在现代汽车中加强供应链安全、代码签名和鲁棒 OTA 验证的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.kasperskycontenthub.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/43/2026/08/21071446/android-head-unit-malware-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://media.kasperskycontenthub.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/43/2026/08/21071446/android-head-unit-malware-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16753' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mathematics in the age of AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 时代理解中的数学</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49362728' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 207</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49362728' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 254</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how AI reshapes mathematical practice, from algorithmic thinking to new tools and proofs, raising questions about rigor and intuition in the era of machine learning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨 AI 如何改变数学研究的实践路径，从算法思维到新工具与证明，提出在机器学习时代对严格性与直觉性的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://martypc.net/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MartyPC is a cross-platform emulator of early PCs written in Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MartyPC：用 Rust 编写的跨平台早期 PC 模拟器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49405816' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 205</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49405816' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MartyPC is a cross-platform emulator for early PCs written in Rust. Its Rust foundation aims for safety, performance, and portability across Windows, macOS, and Linux. The project enables preservation and exploration of vintage software with a modern, well-supported toolchain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MartyPC 是用 Rust 编写的跨平台早期 PC 模拟器。以 Rust 为基础，追求安全、性能和跨平台性，覆盖 Windows、macOS 和 Linux。该项目为复原和体验早期软件提供了一个现代、易维护的工具链。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://martypc.net/assets/marty_logo_about_twitter.png' data-full='https://martypc.net/assets/marty_logo_about_twitter.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49286258' data-tags='Programming,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kolja.rs/algorithm-d/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A decades-old bug in Knuth&#x27;s long division (TAOCP Vol II, Algorithm 4.3.1D)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Knuth 长除法中的几十年前的漏洞（TAOCP 第 II 卷，算法 4.3.1D）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49286258' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 205</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49286258' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A decades-old bug in Knuth&#x27;s long division algorithm (TAOCP Vol II, Algorithm 4.3.1D) has been highlighted, illustrating subtle pitfalls in numeric methods and the risk of relying on classical references without validation. The post underscores the importance of peer review and continuing scrutiny of foundational algorithms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Knuth 的《TAOCP》第二卷算法 4.3.1D 的长除法实现中，长期存在的一个 bug 被揭示，揭示了数值方法中的微妙陷阱以及仅凭经典教材可能带来的风险。该文章强调对基础算法的同行评审和持续审视的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bear-images.sfo2.cdn.digitaloceanspaces.com/kolja/untitled.webp' data-full='https://bear-images.sfo2.cdn.digitaloceanspaces.com/kolja/untitled.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一名德州学生如何揭发一起失控 AI 的黑客企图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49387959' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 204</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49387959' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Texas student uncovered a rogue AI hacking attempt and blew the whistle, triggering investigations and underscoring the growing risk of AI-enabled cyber threats. The incident highlights the importance of student vigilance, security literacy, and responsible disclosure in a landscape of rapid AI-enabled risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名德州学生揭露了一起由 AI 驱动的黑客企图，并通过举报推动调查，凸显了 AI 助力的网络威胁日益增加的风险。事件强调学生的警觉、信息安全素养以及在快速发展的 AI 风险环境中实施负责任披露的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/pg83/solo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Solo – a .so loader for static Linux binaries</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Solo：一个用于静态 Linux 二进制的 .so 加载器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49354613' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 200</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49354613' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 235</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Solo is a small tool that lets you load .so dynamic libraries into static Linux binaries, enabling fully self-contained executables. It simplifies distribution and can improve portability in environments with strict library policies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Solo 是一个小型工具，可将 .so 动态库装入静态 Linux 二进制文件，生成完全集成的可执行文件。它简化了分发并在对库政策严格的环境中提升了可移植性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5351e6014f1c4eb1cd6ee68802995848142a912fac8c5676d4dfb3ad2a5bb6a6/pg83/solo' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5351e6014f1c4eb1cd6ee68802995848142a912fac8c5676d4dfb3ad2a5bb6a6/pg83/solo' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>St. Lucie 核电站1号机手动停机，三根控制棒落入堆芯</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49320856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 194</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49320856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 148</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>St. Lucie Unit 1 was manually shut down after three control rods unexpectedly dropped into the core, triggering shutdown procedures. There were no radiation releases reported, and investigators will examine causes and preventive measures to avoid recurrence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>St. Lucie 核电站1号机因人工停机时有三根控制棒落入堆芯，触发停机程序。尚未发生放射性释放，相关部门将调查原因并评估防止类似事件的改进措施。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lapcatsoftware.com/articles/2026/8/7.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>hdiutil is deprecated in macOS 27 Golden Gate</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>macOS 27 Golden Gate 中弃用 hdiutil</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49402741' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 185</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49402741' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple plans to deprecate hdiutil in macOS 27 Golden Gate, signaling a shift away from legacy disk-image tooling. The change will prompt developers and automation users to migrate to newer Disk Images APIs or alternative tooling, potentially affecting installers and CI pipelines. Expect guidance from Apple on migration timelines and recommended approaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果将在 macOS 27 Golden Gate 中弃用 hdiutil，标志着对旧版磁盘镜像工具的转变。此变动将促使开发者和自动化用户迁移到更新的磁盘镜像 API 或替代工具，可能影响安装程序和持续集成流程。预计苹果将提供迁移时间表和推荐做法。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49300272' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 173</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49300272' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Activation energy is proposed as a useful modeling metaphor beyond chemistry, applied to technology adoption, learning dynamics, and system bottlenecks. The piece argues that barrier-like models can explain when and why transitions happen, offering a simple lens for complex processes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章提出激活能量作为化学之外的有用建模隐喻，适用于技术采用、学习动力学以及系统瓶颈等场景。作者认为将转变看作跨越能垒的过程，可以用简单的模型解释何时、为何会发生转换，从而为复杂现象提供直观的分析框架。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49322573' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://buf.build/blog/protobuf-lsp' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Protobuf has LSP support</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Protobuf 已支持 LSP（语言服务器协议）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49322573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 172</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49322573' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Protobuf now has built-in LSP support, enabling editors to provide autocompletion, diagnostics, and refactoring for protobuf schemas. That reduces friction for developers who model APIs and data contracts, potentially speeding up schema evolution and integration across services. This aligns protobuf with other IDLs bridging IDE tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Protobuf 现在原生支持 LSP（语言服务器协议），让编辑器能够对 protobuf 架构提供自动补全、诊断和重构等功能，减少 API 与数据契约建模时的摩擦，助力跨服务的模式演进与集成。这也使 protobuf 在 IDE 工具链方面与其他 IDL 看齐。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://buf.build/images/cms/blog/69c3092bd98bec6b03e4d7e5_blog-protobuf-finally-has.png' data-full='https://buf.build/images/cms/blog/69c3092bd98bec6b03e4d7e5_blog-protobuf-finally-has.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49355968' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://terrytao.wordpress.com/2026/08/18/palomar-a-registry-of-lean-verified-mathematics/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Palomar: A registry of Lean verified mathematics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Palomar：Lean 验证数学的注册库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49355968' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49355968' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Palomar is a registry that catalogs mathematics proven in Lean, enabling researchers to reference machine-checked results with confidence. It helps track formalized theorems across libraries and fosters collaboration between mathematicians and proof assistants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Palomar 是一个收录 Lean 验证数学定理的注册库，便于研究者对机器可验证的结果进行可信引用。它帮助在各个库之间跟踪形式化定理，促进数学家与证明助手的协作。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://secure.gravatar.com/blavatar/bd4bda4207561b6998f10dec44b570f04ff4072b20f89162d525b186dfca3e49?s=200&amp;ts=1787122201' data-full='https://secure.gravatar.com/blavatar/bd4bda4207561b6998f10dec44b570f04ff4072b20f89162d525b186dfca3e49?s=200&amp;ts=1787122201' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49336304' data-tags='Business,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c8xnwqe00v1o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>India has paved the way for charging merchants a fee on UPI transactions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>印度为向UPI交易向商家收费铺路</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49336304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49336304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 213</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>India&#x27;s policy signals that merchants can be charged fees on UPI payments, potentially shifting the economics of digital transactions. Expect impact on merchant acceptance dynamics, competitive pricing, and user experience as payment rails evolve.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>印度放开对 UPI 交易向商户收费的路径，将改变数字支付的成本结构。对商户接受度、定价竞争和用户体验等方面都会产生影响，支付体系也因此在演化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/250b/live/07d59750-96ed-11f1-bdf3-57bf9a99cb46.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/250b/live/07d59750-96ed-11f1-bdf3-57bf9a99cb46.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49410097' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://reinvently.co.uk/tools/ed-o-meter/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLM-5.3 (open-weight) beat Anthropic/OpenAI models – for 1/5 the cost</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源权重的 GLM-5.3 以五分之一成本击败 Anthropic/OpenAI 模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49410097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 170</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49410097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open-weight GLM-5.3 reportedly outperforms proprietary Anthropic and OpenAI models while running at about one-fifth the cost. The finding underscores how open models are narrowing the cost gap for inference-heavy workloads and could reshape tooling choices for AI developers and teams. Open-weight models are increasingly competitive on performance-per-dollar.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称，开源权重的 GLM-5.3 在性能上优于Anthropic与OpenAI的模型，而成本大约只有五分之一。此发现凸显开源模型在高推理成本工作负载中的性价比正在缩小，可能改变AI开发者与团队的工具选择。开源权重模型在性价比方面正变得越来越有竞争力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://reinvently.co.uk/images/hero-ed-o-meter-leaderboard.jpg' data-full='https://reinvently.co.uk/images/hero-ed-o-meter-leaderboard.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Calls for extensible software architectures that can adapt to evolving LLM capabilities; discusses plugin ecosystems, safety boundaries, and governance to maintain agility in product suites.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nari-labs.com/blog/qwen3-tts-speed-cost-frontier/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How we made a text-to-speech model respond in sub-50 ms</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们如何让文本转语音模型在50毫秒内响应</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49389952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49389952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A detailed account of achieving sub-50ms TTS responsiveness, discussing optimizations, model design, and hardware considerations. The result enables near-instantaneous speech synthesis, important for interactive apps and real-time assistants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>详细介绍实现文本转语音响应低于50毫秒的做法，涵盖优化、模型设计与硬件考虑。这一性能使对话式应用和实时语音助手的交互更为即时。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://atproto.com/blog/atproto-spaces-alpha' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ATProto spaces: A new extension to ATProto that enables non-public data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ATProto Spaces：一个新扩展，支持非公开数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49379732' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49379732' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ATProto Spaces Alpha introduces a data access layer enabling non-public data within the ATProto ecosystem, allowing private spaces and restricted sharing. This marks a move toward more versatile, privacy-conscious social apps built on decentralized infrastructure, while raising questions about moderation, access control, and data sovereignty.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ATProto Spaces Alpha 引入在 ATProto 生态中处理非公开数据的访问层，支持私有空间和受限共享。这标志着在去中心化基础设施上打造更具隐私意识的社交应用的趋势，同时引发对内容监管、访问控制和数据主权等问题的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://atproto.com/-/blog/atproto-spaces-alpha/opengraph-image.png?18114f605119ecc9' data-full='https://atproto.com/-/blog/atproto-spaces-alpha/opengraph-image.png?18114f605119ecc9' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49389565' data-tags='AI,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(201) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://canews24.online/?p=71' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI boosted homework scores, then exam scores dropped: Study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 提升作业分数，考试分数却下降：研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49389565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 165</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49389565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study found that AI-assisted homework boosted scores, but exam results dropped, suggesting AI helps practice but may impair deep understanding or test-taking under pressure. The findings raise questions about how to integrate AI into learning while preserving learning outcomes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项研究发现，AI 辅助作业得分提升，但考试分数却下降，提示 AI 有助于练习但可能削弱深度理解或应试能力。研究引发对在学习中合适引入 AI 的讨论，以及如何保护学习成效。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://canews24.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/cropped-ChatGPT-Image-Aug-20-2026-07_36_21-AM-1.png' data-full='https://canews24.online/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/cropped-ChatGPT-Image-Aug-20-2026-07_36_21-AM-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(202) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.acm.org/articles/people-of-acm/2026/russ-cox' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>People of ACM – Russ Cox</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ACM 人物：Russ Cox</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49327408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 163</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49327408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ACM profiles Russ Cox, a prominent software engineer known for Go language and systems research, highlighting his contributions to programming language communities and open source.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ACM 专栏聚焦计算学领军人物 Russ Cox，回顾他在 Go 语言及系统编程领域的贡献、研究风格与对开源社区的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49340299' data-tags='Science,Data,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danluu.com/benchpocalypse/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Benchmarkpocalypse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基准灾难</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49340299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 160</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49340299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Benchmarkpocalypse argues that the current flood of benchmarks across hardware and AI has produced a noisy, unreliable landscape, with cherry-picked results and non-reproducible setups becoming common. It highlights how decision-makers can be misled by vanity numbers and calls for stricter standards, standardized workloads, and transparent methodologies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基准测评的激增导致结果噪声大、难以重复，常见的是挑选性数据与不可复现的测试。对决策者而言，这些 vanity numbers 可能误导判断，文章呼吁建立更严格的基准标准、统一工作负载与透明的方法论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(204) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grist.org/transportation/californias-new-tire-efficiency-rules-could-save-drivers-1b-a-year/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>California&#x27;s new tire efficiency rules could save drivers $1B a year</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加州新轮胎能效法规每年可为司机省下10亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49340710' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 160</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49340710' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>California&#x27;s tire efficiency rules could save drivers about $1B annually by reducing rolling resistance. The policy underscores ongoing efforts to improve vehicle efficiency and could foreshadow broader adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州的新轮胎能效规则通过降低滚阻，预计每年可为司机节省约10亿美元。政策凸显持续推动车辆高效化的趋势，也可能为未来更广泛的采用铺路。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/nih-ending-key-grant-budding-clinical-researchers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NIH is ending a key grant for budding clinical researchers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NIH 结束面向新兴临床研究者的关键资助</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49321353' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49321353' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NIH is ending a key grant that supported aspiring clinical researchers. The move risks thinning the pipeline of early-career clinician-scientists and may force institutions to pivot training programs or seek alternative funding, potentially slowing translational research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NIH 将停止一项支持初级临床研究者的关键资助，可能削弱早期临床研究人员的培养通道。机构可能被迫重新分配经费或调整培训计划，若缺乏新的替代支持，或会拖慢转化研究的进展。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>低技术陶瓷水过滤器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49259980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49259980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A simple, field-ready ceramic water filter design uses porous ceramic elements to remove bacteria and particulates, offering a low-cost solution for clean water in resource-poor settings. It’s durable, easy to manufacture, and widely used in humanitarian contexts, with maintenance focused on cleaning and replacing worn filters.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种简单、可在现场使用的陶瓷水过滤器通过多孔陶瓷元件去除细菌和颗粒，适用于资源匮乏地区的低成本净水方案。该设计耐用、易于制造，在人道主义场景中广泛应用，维护重点在于清洁与更换磨损的滤芯。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(211) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://matklad.github.io/2026/08/06/neat-io-threaded.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zig’s Io.Threaded is neat</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zig 的 io.threaded：简洁而强大的并发 I/O 方案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49388694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49388694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look at Zig&#x27;s io.threaded pattern, which enables safe, concurrent I/O without heavy garbage collection, aligning with Zig&#x27;s philosophy of minimal runtime and explicit control. The approach appeals to systems programmers who want predictable performance and low overhead.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文介绍 Zig 的 io.threaded 模式，展示在无重垃圾回收的前提下实现安全并发 I/O 的简洁方法。该做法体现了 Zig 的最小运行时与显式控制理念，深受需要可预测性能、低开销的系统开发者欢迎。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49409780' data-tags='Energy,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(213) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://studyfinds.com/coconut-oil-jet-fuel-matches-kerosenes-efficiency-in-engine-tests/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Coconut oil jet fuel matches kerosene&#x27;s efficiency in engine tests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>椰子油喷气燃料在发动机测试中与煤油效率相当</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49409780' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 153</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49409780' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 184</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tests show coconut-oil-based jet fuel delivering comparable efficiency to kerosene in engines, signaling a potential sustainable alternative for aviation fuel. However, questions remain about scalability, supply chains, and lifecycle emissions. If scalable, natural-feedstock fuels could diversify aviation fuel options.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>发动机测试显示以椰子油为基础的喷气燃料在效率上与煤油相当，提示其可能成为航空燃料的可持续替代方案。但关于规模化、供应链和全生命周期排放等仍有待解决；若可规模化，天然原料燃料能为航空燃料选项带来更多样性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://studyfinds.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/Coconut-oil.jpg?ver=1787499183' data-full='https://studyfinds.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/Coconut-oil.jpg?ver=1787499183' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49323932' data-tags='Politics,Culture,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(215) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://futurism.com/artificial-intelligence/young-people-ai-ceos-executives-poll' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Young People Hate AI CEOs So Passionately That It&#x27;s Almost Hard to Believe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>年轻人对 AI CEO 的强烈反感，几乎让人难以置信</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49323932' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49323932' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 182</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A poll finds younger demographics vehemently oppose AI company leaders, reflecting distrust in AI governance, ethics, and market power. The data suggests youth want more transparency and accountability from AI firms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>调查显示年轻人对AI公司高层强烈反感，反映出对AI治理、伦理与权力集中的不信任。数据表明年轻人希望企业在透明度和问责方面有更高标准。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(216) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.computer.org/csdl/magazine/mi/2021/06/09623402/1yJTvlRLmhi' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Z80 – The 1970s Microprocessor Still Alive (2021)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Z80：1970年代微处理器仍然存活（2021）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49398158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 150</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49398158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Z80 microprocessor, though from the 1970s, remains influential in education and hobbyist circles thanks to its approachable instruction set and broad ecosystem. The article surveys why it still matters for retro computing, embedded projects, and teaching concepts like assembly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Z80 微处理器虽然诞生于 1970 年代，但在教育和爱好者社区中仍具影响力，因其指令集简单、生态广泛。本文探讨了为何它在复古计算、嵌入式项目及汇编教学等方面仍有价值。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A bug in Codex on AWS Bedrock caused a tenfold increase in charges, highlighting hidden costs in AI services. The piece suggests better monitoring, budget alerts, and careful usage patterns when leveraging hosted AI APIs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Codex 在 AWS Bedrock 上的一个 bug 导致账单暴增十倍，暴露出托管式 AI 服务的隐藏成本。建议设定预算告警、加强监控并谨慎使用相关 API。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(221) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://brandur.org/fragments/postgres-without-pgbouncer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Does anyone run Postgres without PgBouncer?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>有人在不使用 PgBouncer 的情况下运行 Postgres 吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49277952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 147</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49277952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Some teams run Postgres directly without PgBouncer, arguing that modern connection pools and well-tuned workloads can avoid the extra hop.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一些团队直接在 Postgres 上工作，不使用 PgBouncer，认为在经过优化的并发场景中可以避免额外的连接池开销。文中讨论连接开销、连接池生命周期，以及在高并发环境中 PgBouncer 是否仍具备价值。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(222) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://encore.dev/blog/firecracker-apple-silicon' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We Rebuilt the Linux MicroVM Stack on Apple Silicon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Apple Silicon 上重建 Linux MicroVM 架构</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Encore.dev details rebuilding a Linux microVM stack (Firecracker-like) to run efficiently on Apple Silicon, highlighting performance gains and architectural tweaks. This work demonstrates feasibility of cloud-native VMs on ARM-based Macs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Encore.dev 介绍在 Apple Silicon 上重建 Linux 微型虚拟机栈（类似 Firecracker），强调性能提升与架构调整。这项工作展示了在 ARM Mac 上运行云原生虚拟机的可行性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(223) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://punyamishra.com/2026/04/16/why-sal-khant-on-learning-by-making-but-teaching-by-telling/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Sal Khan&#x27;t: On Learning by Making but Teaching by Telling</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>萨尔·汗为何：学习要通过动手实践，但教学却偏向讲授</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49409862' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 143</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49409862' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues for learning-by-doing and constructing knowledge through making, not just being told. It critiques over-reliance on lectures and advocates balancing exploration with explicit explanation to improve retention and transfer. The takeaway is that education benefits from hands-on practice paired with thoughtful guidance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张通过动手实践来学习、通过做中建构知识，而不仅仅是被告知。它批评对讲授的过度依赖，提倡将探索与清晰讲解相结合，以提升记忆与迁移能力。核心观点是教育应在动手练习与引导性讲解之间取得平衡。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(224) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medicine.washu.edu/news/how-early-life-stress-leaves-a-scar-inside-brain-cells/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Early-life stress leaves a &#x27;scar&#x27; inside brain cells in mice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>早期压力在脑细胞内部留下疤痕</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49380303' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 142</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49380303' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New findings show that early-life stress can imprint lasting cellular-level changes in the brain, with potential implications for psychiatric risk and resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新发现表明，早期压力会在脑细胞层面留下持久的改变，可能影响精神疾病风险与个体韧性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://medicine.washu.edu/app/uploads/2026/08/Creed-spring-take-2.gif' data-full='https://medicine.washu.edu/app/uploads/2026/08/Creed-spring-take-2.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49272891' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(225) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/d41586-026-02498-1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A particle made of force: physicists say they&#x27;ve found mysterious &#x27;glueball&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>由力构成的粒子：物理学家称发现神秘的胶球</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49272891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 142</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49272891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Physicists report evidence for a glueball, a bound state of gluons with no quark content, a long-sought prediction of quantum chromodynamics. If confirmed, it would deepen understanding of how the strong force binds matter.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>物理学家报道发现无夸克组成的胶球的证据，这是一种由胶子组成的束缚态，长期被量子色动力学所预言。若得到确证，将深化对强相互作用如何结合物质的理解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-02498-1/d41586-026-02498-1_53111266.jpg' data-full='https://media.nature.com/lw1200/magazine-assets/d41586-026-02498-1/d41586-026-02498-1_53111266.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49395605' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(226) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grapheneos.social/@GrapheneOS/117136278553665985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Initial focus for our partnership with Motorola is a regular non-folding device</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>与 Motorola 的初步合作聚焦于常规非折叠设备</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49395605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49395605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The initial focus of the GrapheneOS-Motorola partnership targets a standard non-folding device, signaling emphasis on security-privacy custom ROMs for typical devices rather than foldables.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>双方合作的初步重点是为常规非折叠设备提供更强的隐私与安全性定制系统，强调对普通设备的支持。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49376769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 137</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49376769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that assembly language can be typed and that safety can be improved with typed inline assembly or typed assembly language ideas. It discusses design choices that enhance correctness without sacrificing low-level control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张汇编语言也可以具备类型系统，通过对内联汇编或类型化汇编语言的设计，提升安全性与正确性，同时不牺牲底层控制力。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI medical scribe made an error during a patient appointment, leaving the patient devastated and underscoring reliability concerns for AI-assisted clinical tools. The incident highlights the need for robust validation, human oversight, and fallback processes in AI medical workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 医疗记录助手在就诊过程中出错，造成患者情绪崩溃，凸显了 AI 医疗工具在可靠性方面的担忧。事件强调在 AI 就诊工作流中需要严格验证、人工监督与回退机制。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49368625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 134</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49368625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gardner&#x27;s police department discontinued Flock&#x27;s cameras amid privacy and surveillance concerns around license plate readers. The move reflects growing scrutiny of automated surveillance and its civil liberties implications, plus thorny questions about public safety vs privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gardner 警方宣布停止使用 Flock 摄像头，聚焦对车牌识别等监控技术的隐私和公民自由担忧。这一决定体现了社会对自动化监控的日益审查以及公共安全与隐私之间的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49285096' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(234) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pikuma.com/blog/programming-intel-pentium-mmx-simd' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SIMD in the 90s: Programming Intel&#x27;s Pentium MMX</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>90年代的 SIMD：在英特尔 Pentium MMX 上的编程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49285096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 134</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49285096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on 1990s SIMD shows how Intel&#x27;s Pentium MMX introduced integer SIMD on x86, but with design constraints that limited portability and performance. The piece explains how MMX influenced later generations (SSE/AVX) and highlights early compiler and ISA trade-offs that still matter for modern vectorization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 90 年代的 SIMD，介绍英特尔 Pentium MMX 如何在 x86 上引入整型向量指令，以及设计约束带来的可移植性与性能局限。文中阐述 MMX 如何影响了后续的 SSE/AVX，以及早期编译器与 ISA 的权衡，这些对如今的矢量化仍具启示作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pikuma.com/images/blog/programming-intel-pentium-mmx-simd/thumbnail-1200x630.jpg' data-full='https://pikuma.com/images/blog/programming-intel-pentium-mmx-simd/thumbnail-1200x630.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49347477' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(237) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/vixhal-baraiya/microgpt-c' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MicroGPT-C in pure C hits 10M TPS on Apple M5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纯 C 实现的 MicroGPT 在苹果 M5 上达到 1000 万 tps</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49347477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 127</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49347477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pure-C implementation of MicroGPT achieves 10 million tokens per second on Apple M5 hardware, underscoring impressive runtime efficiency and the potential for lightweight AI inference on consumer devices. This demonstrates how language models can be ported to low-level, high-performance stacks, enabling edge AI use cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纯 C 实现的 MicroGPT 在苹果 M5 硬件上达到 1,000 万 tokens/秒，凸显低级语言实现下的高效推理能力，点亮边缘 AI 的潜力。此成就展示了将语言模型移植到高性能、低开销的栈上的可行性，便于在本地设备部署。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a9cfc92bf9ff965cf03a8d7cd7c5ce00d376870de3863abc887d79b65f9d3396/vixhal-baraiya/microgpt-c' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a9cfc92bf9ff965cf03a8d7cd7c5ce00d376870de3863abc887d79b65f9d3396/vixhal-baraiya/microgpt-c' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(238) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ixuvo.com/blog/tigerbeetle-core-system-architecture-performance-engineering' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TigerBeetle Core System Architecture: Deconstructing Performance Engineering</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TigerBeetle 核心系统架构：性能工程的解构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49386659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49386659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TigerBeetle&#x27;s core system architecture is dissected to reveal performance engineering strategies in a high-throughput database project. Key ideas include zero-copy design, lock-free structures, and careful memory management to minimize latency. The piece offers practical lessons for building low-latency financial-grade systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TigerBeetle 的核心系统架构被拆解，展示高吞吐数据库项目中的性能工程策略。核心思路包括零拷贝设计、无锁结构和精细的内存管理，以减少延迟，为构建低延迟金融级系统提供可操作的经验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ixuvo.com/api/pb/api/files/posts/huy107e0jp78h5b/tigerbeetle_core_system_architecture_performance_engineering_cover_rsy2ravvpk.jpg' data-full='https://ixuvo.com/api/pb/api/files/posts/huy107e0jp78h5b/tigerbeetle_core_system_architecture_performance_engineering_cover_rsy2ravvpk.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(241) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://meanhamster.com/news/agi-64-brings-sierra-adventures-to-the-commodore-64' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AGI-64 Brings Sierra Adventures to the Commodore 64</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AGI-64 把 Sierra 冒险带进 Commodore 64</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49325714' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49325714' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AGI-64 ports Sierra&#x27;s AGI-era adventure games to the Commodore 64 by reimplementing the engine for the classic hardware. The project demonstrates how modern tooling can revive beloved titles on constrained machines and highlight retro-game preservation challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AGI-64 通过为 Commodore 64 重新实现 AGI 引擎，将 Sierra 的 AGI 时代冒险游戏移植上去。该项目展示了现代工具如何让经典作品在受限硬件上重现，同时揭示了复古游戏保存的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49363665' data-tags='DevOps,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(242) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ngrok.com/blog/probes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Kubernetes Probes Work</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kubernetes 探针工作原理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49363665' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 125</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49363665' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kubernetes probes determine whether containers are ready or healthy, influencing pod lifecycle and rollout behavior. Probes like liveness, readiness, and startup probes help detect failures and avoid traffic routing to unhealthy pods, but misconfig can cause unnecessary restarts or slow deployments. The piece likely explains practical tuning tips and pitfalls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kubernetes 探针用来判断容器是否就绪或健康，直接影响 Pod 的生命周期和滚动更新策略。通过存活、就绪和启动探针，系统可以在发现故障时隔离或重新调度，但配置不当会导致不必要的重启或部署变慢。本文提供了实际的调优要点与常见坑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ngrok.com/blog-assets/images/2026-08-19-probes/og.jpg' data-full='https://ngrok.com/blog-assets/images/2026-08-19-probes/og.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将一个 Git 提交拆分为多个提交</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A guide to splitting a large Git commit into smaller, logical commits for better history and review. Practical commands and tips help maintain traceability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍如何将一个大型提交拆分为若干个更易理解的提交，以提升提交历史的可追溯性和审查效率，配以实用命令与要点。</p>
